/**
 * ============================================================================
 * PACE Engine v2
 * Author: Icehellionx
 * Role: Topic / Beat (Director) CNN classifier for RP chatbots.
 *
 * High-level:
 *   - Loads a quantized CNN model from getRawModelData().
 *   - Tokenizes the latest user message.
 *   - Runs a forward pass through:
 *       * Embedding layer
 *       * 1D CNN with kernels [3, 4, 5] + max-pool + ReLU
 *       * Two softmax heads:
 *           - Topic (primary)
 *           - Beat  (secondary)
 *   - Appends a [PACE]...[/PACE] tag line into context.character.scenario
 *     describing top Topic and Beat predictions.
 *
 * NOTE: Logic and behavior are preserved exactly from the original version.
 *       Only formatting, comments, and naming/log labels have been cleaned up.
 * ============================================================================
 */

(function () {
    'use strict';

    // ============================================================================
    // DIRECTOR / PACE ENGINE CONFIG
    // ============================================================================
    // DEBUG: when set to 1, logs the PACE output line to the console.
    var DEBUG = 1;

    // Maximum number of tokens processed by the CNN.
    // Must match the JSON model / trainer configuration.
    var MAX_TOKENS = 48;

    // ============================================================================
    // SAFE LOGGING
    // ============================================================================
    // Wrap console.log in a try/catch so environments without a console don't break.
    function safeLog(msg) {
        try {
            if (console && console.log) console.log(msg);
        } catch (e) { }
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // MODEL INIT
    // ============================================================================
    var MODEL = null;             // Holds the fully initialized model once loaded.
    var MODEL_INIT_ERROR = false; // Prevents repeated init attempts after a failure.

    function initModel() {
        // If we already initialized (or failed), skip re-initialization.
        if (MODEL || MODEL_INIT_ERROR) return;

        var raw = "";
        try {
            raw = getRawModelData();
        } catch (e) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            return;
        }

        if (!raw || raw.length < 10) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            safeLog("[PACE] Model: Empty raw data.");
            return;
        }

        var parsed;
        try {
            parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        } catch (e) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            safeLog("[PACE] JSON parse error: " + e.message);
            return;
        }

        try {
            var w = parsed.weights || {};
            var s = parsed.scales || {};

            // Dequantize a flat or nested weight/bias array using its scalar scale.
            function deq(vals, scale) {
                if (!vals || scale == null) return null;

                // Handle flattened or nested arrays.
                var flat = Array.isArray(vals[0]) ? vals.flat(Infinity) : vals;
                var out = new Float32Array(flat.length);

                for (var i = 0; i < flat.length; i++) {
                    out[i] = flat[i] * scale;
                }
                return out;
            }

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            // Convolutional layers: kernel sizes [3, 4, 5]
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            var convs = [];
            var K = [3, 4, 5]; // kernel sizes

            for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                var wKey = "c" + i + "w";
                var bKey = "c" + i + "b";
                var weight = deq(w[wKey], s[wKey]);
                var bias = deq(w[bKey], s[bKey]);

                if (!weight || !bias) {
                    throw new Error("Missing weights for conv layer " + i);
                }

                convs.push({
                    w: weight,
                    b: bias,
                    k: K[i]
                });
            }

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            // Vocabulary map
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            var vocabMap = {};
            if (Array.isArray(parsed.vocab)) {
                for (var j = 0; j < parsed.vocab.length; j++) {
                    vocabMap[parsed.vocab[j]] = j;
                }
            }

            // Fallback ID for unknown tokens (defaults to 1 if <unk> missing).
            var unkId = (vocabMap["<unk>"] !== undefined) ? vocabMap["<unk>"] : 1;

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            // KEY MAPPING FIX
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            // Check for new trainer keys (fc_topic_w) OR old keys (rel_w), etc.
            var topicW = deq(w.fc_topic_w || w.rel_w, s.fc_topic_w || s.rel_w);
            var topicB = deq(w.fc_topic_b || w.rel_b, s.fc_topic_b || s.rel_b);

            var beatW = deq(w.fc_beat_w || w.pow_w, s.fc_beat_w || s.pow_w);
            var beatB = deq(w.fc_beat_b || w.pow_b, s.fc_beat_b || s.pow_b);

            if (!topicW || !topicB || !beatW || !beatB) {
                throw new Error("Missing classification head weights.");
            }

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            // Embedding table and meta
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
            var embedding = deq(w.emb_text || w.emb, s.emb_text || s.emb);
            if (!embedding) throw new Error("Missing embedding table.");

            MODEL = {
                vocabMap: vocabMap,
                unkId: unkId,
                vocabSize: parsed.vocab.length,
                embedDim: parsed.meta.embed_dim || 16,
                convs: convs,

                embedding: embedding,

                // Topic head
                topicW: topicW,
                topicB: topicB,

                // Beat head
                beatW: beatW,
                beatB: beatB,

                // Label sets
                topicLabels: parsed.topic_classes || parsed.relation_labels || [],
                beatLabels: parsed.beat_classes || parsed.power_labels || [],

                // Class counts
                numTopic: (parsed.topic_classes || []).length,
                numBeat: (parsed.beat_classes || []).length
            };

            safeLog("[PACE] Init success. Vocab: " + MODEL.vocabSize);
        } catch (e) {
            MODEL_INIT_ERROR = true;
            safeLog("[PACE] Init failed: " + e.message);
        }
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // TOKENIZER — NO NORMALIZATION, NO LOWERCASE, NO CHARACTER FILTERING
    // ============================================================================
    // This version keeps the text exactly as the user typed it.
    // It only splits on whitespace and truncates to MAX_TOKENS.
    function cnn_tokenize(text) {
        if (!text) return [];
        return text
            .split(/\s+/)        // split only on whitespace
            .filter(Boolean)     // remove empty strings
            .slice(0, MAX_TOKENS);
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // CONV1D MAX + RELU
    // ============================================================================
    // Computes a 1D convolution over time, applies ReLU, then global max-pools.
    //
    // inputCHW: Float32Array with shape (C, L), flattened (channel-first).
    // weight   : Float32Array for conv kernels.
    // bias     : Float32Array for conv biases.
    // C        : number of channels (embedding dim).
    // L        : sequence length (MAX_TOKENS).
    // k        : kernel size.
    function conv1d_max_relu(inputCHW, weight, bias, C, L, k) {
        if (!weight || !bias) return new Float32Array(0);

        var O = bias.length;          // number of filters
        var out = new Float32Array(O);
        var pad = (k - 1) >> 1;         // symmetric padding

        for (var f = 0; f < O; f++) {
            var maxVal = -Infinity;
            var wOff = f * C * k;

            for (var t = 0; t < L; t++) {
                var s = bias[f];

                for (var c = 0; c < C; c++) {
                    var baseInput = c * L;
                    var baseWeight = wOff + c * k;

                    for (var j = 0; j < k; j++) {
                        var idx = t + j - pad;
                        if (idx >= 0 && idx < L) {
                            s += inputCHW[baseInput + idx] * weight[baseWeight + j];
                        }
                    }
                }

                // ReLU activation.
                var relu_s = s > 0 ? s : 0;
                if (relu_s > maxVal) maxVal = relu_s;
            }

            out[f] = maxVal;
        }

        return out;
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // FORWARD PASS
    // ============================================================================
    // Runs a full forward pass over the input text and returns:
    //   {
    //     topic: Float32Array(numTopic), // topic probabilities
    //     beat:  Float32Array(numBeat)   // beat probabilities
    //   }
    function cnn_predict(text) {
        initModel();
        if (!MODEL) return null;

        // Tokenize text and convert to vocab IDs.
        var tokens = cnn_tokenize(text);
        var ids = tokens.map(function (t) {
            return (MODEL.vocabMap[t] !== undefined) ? MODEL.vocabMap[t] : MODEL.unkId;
        });

        // Right-pad with zeros.
        while (ids.length < MAX_TOKENS) {
            ids.push(0);
        }

        var C = MODEL.embedDim;
        var T = MAX_TOKENS;

        // Embedding lookup: CHW layout [channel][time].
        var embedded = new Float32Array(C * T);
        for (var t = 0; t < T; t++) {
            var id = ids[t];
            var off = id * C;
            for (var c = 0; c < C; c++) {
                embedded[c * T + t] = MODEL.embedding[off + c];
            }
        }

        // Convolutional layers + max-pool over time.
        var feats = [];
        var total = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < MODEL.convs.length; i++) {
            var cv = MODEL.convs[i];
            var res = conv1d_max_relu(embedded, cv.w, cv.b, C, T, cv.k);
            feats.push(res);
            total += res.length;
        }

        // Concatenate feature maps from each kernel size into a flat vector.
        var flat = new Float32Array(total);
        var ptr = 0;
        for (var k = 0; k < feats.length; k++) {
            flat.set(feats[k], ptr);
            ptr += feats[k].length;
        }

        // Generic forward pass for a single softmax classification head.
        function forwardHead(W, B, n) {
            var logits = new Float32Array(n);

            // Compute logits = W @ flat + B
            for (var r = 0; r < n; r++) {
                var sum = B[r];
                var base = r * total;
                for (var q = 0; q < total; q++) {
                    sum += W[base + q] * flat[q];
                }
                logits[r] = sum;
            }

            var out = new Float32Array(n);
            var maxL = -Infinity;

            // Numerically stable softmax
            for (var b = 0; b < n; b++) {
                if (logits[b] > maxL) maxL = logits[b];
            }

            var sumExp = 0;
            for (var d = 0; d < n; d++) {
                out[d] = Math.exp(logits[d] - maxL);
                sumExp += out[d];
            }

            for (var e = 0; e < n; e++) {
                out[e] /= sumExp || 1; // avoid division by zero
            }

            return out;
        }

        return {
            topic: forwardHead(MODEL.topicW, MODEL.topicB, MODEL.numTopic),
            beat: forwardHead(MODEL.beatW, MODEL.beatB, MODEL.numBeat)
        };
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // INFERENCE LOOP
    // ============================================================================
    // Integrates with the host environment:
    //   - Reads last message from context.chat.last_messages
    //   - Runs cnn_predict()
    //   - Appends a [PACE]...[/PACE] summary line to context.character.scenario
    if (typeof context !== "object" || !context) return;

    context.character = context.character || {};

    var msgs = (context.chat && context.chat.last_messages) || [];
    var last = msgs.length ? msgs[msgs.length - 1].message : "";

    if (last) {
        try {
            var res = cnn_predict(last);
            if (res && MODEL) {
                // Format the top 2 classes (by probability) into "label:XX%" strings.
                function fmtTopTwo(labels, arr) {
                    if (!labels || !labels.length) return "";

                    var pairs = [];
                    for (var i2 = 0; i2 < labels.length; i2++) {
                        pairs.push({
                            label: labels[i2],
                            prob: arr[i2]
                        });
                    }

                    pairs.sort(function (a, b) {
                        return b.prob - a.prob;
                    });

                    var top = pairs.slice(0, 2);
                    return top.map(function (p) {
                        return p.label + ":" + Math.round(p.prob * 100) + "%";
                    }).join(", ");
                }

                var topicStr = fmtTopTwo(MODEL.topicLabels, res.topic);
                var beatStr = fmtTopTwo(MODEL.beatLabels, res.beat);

                var line = "[PACE]" + topicStr + " | " + beatStr + "[/PACE]";

                if (DEBUG) safeLog(line);

                context.character.scenario += line;
            }
        } catch (err) {
            safeLog("[PACE Error] " + err.message);
        }
    }

    // ============================================================================
    // PASTE JSON MODEL DATA HERE
    // ============================================================================
    // Returns the quantized JSON string for the CNN model.
    // Insert your trained model JSON into the join() array.
    function getRawModelData() {
        return [
            // PASTE THE CONTENT OF director_model.json HERE
            '{"model_type": "cnn_director_v1", "meta": {"vocab_size": 832, "embed_dim": 24, "filters": 48}, "vocab": ["<pad>", "<unk>", "the", "i", "you", "it", "is", "to", "this", "think", "s", "at",
